ts`, `core/realtime*` (every fence is a sibling's); `help/`, `docs/v4/`
(P4.D360); any crate. Every hunk of yours that lands in a sibling's file is a
`HANDOFF:` row in the table below — the sibling applies it; you never edit
the file.

## Survey-verified starting points (2026-10-29, at `236c04e35`; v5 `main` `f6cdceedd`, SPA 0.5.907)

### v4 at the pin — the primitives you port (read each file whole before writing its twin)

- **`hooks/useDialogFocus.ts`** (75 lines at the pin — `58120a7fe` created it,
  `dff76ebef` moved the opener capture to render time): `FOCUSABLE` `:5-6` =
  `'button:not([disabled]), [href], input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'`;
  the opener is read DURING RENDER (`:22-35` — "by the time an effect runs,
  an `autoFocus` field inside the dialog has already taken focus, and the
  opener would be lost"); on open a `requestAnimationFrame` focuses the
  dialog UNLESS it already `contains(document.activeElement)` (`:37-42`);
  the cleanup cancels the frame and refocuses the opener if `isConnected`
  (`:43-46`); the returned keydown handler (`:49-74`): Tab only; zero
  focusables → `preventDefault()` and return; `Shift+Tab` from the FIRST
  focusable OR FROM THE DIALOG ITSELF (`active === dialog`) wraps to last;
  `Tab` from last wraps to first. The dialog element needs `tabIndex={-1}`
  (`:16`).
- **`components/ui/BaseModal.tsx`** (`:96` `closeOnEscape = true`; `:99`
  `const titleId = useId()`; `:100` `useDialogFocus(modalRef, isOpen)`;
  `:102-105` `useClickOutside(modalRef, onClose, { enabled: isOpen &&
  closeOnClickOutside, onEscape: closeOnEscape ? onClose : undefined })`;
  `:113-121` the card `role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby=
  {titleId} tabIndex={-1} onKeyDown={handleFocusKeyDown}`; `:123` `<h2
  id={titleId} className="qt-dialog-title">`; `:124-133` the close button
  `aria-label="Close"`). Escape semantics come from **`hooks/useClickOutside.
  ts:98-116`**: a DOCUMENT `keydown` listener (bubble phase unless
  `escapeCapture`), `event.key === 'Escape'`, returns early when
  `isInFloatingLayer(event.target)` (`:101-103` — P4.D359's layer), no
  `stopPropagation` (so two stacked BaseModals BOTH close on one Escape —
  port that faithfully; the inner-popover `escapeCapture` form is a
  per-host opt-in v5 hosts already emulate with their own listeners).
- **The hosts v4 GATES** (`git grep -n 'closeOnEscape=' 236c04e35 -- app

## Tiered deliverables

Every item is red-first: measure the current shape (the spec case fails, or
the census / grep names the offender) BEFORE the edit, record the count, then
land. Every string in backticks is v4's byte. "Owner's file" means a
`HANDOFF:` row, never an edit.

### Tier 1 — must land

1. **The FIRST commit — the toast live region + every selector it breaks
   (cherry-pickable; its subject names "the toast role").** In
   `ui/toast-container.ts:26` the container becomes `<div role="status"
   aria-live="polite" data-toast-container class="qt-toast-container">`
   (v4's three attributes; the comment `// Polite live region: screen
   readers announce each toast as it lands.` carried as a template or
   docblock comment); each toast `:28` gains `[attr.role]="toast.type ===
   'error' ? 'alert' : null"` with v4's comment `Errors interrupt; everything
   else waits its turn in the polite region.`; the `:20` docblock sentence
   "`role="toast-container"` is v4's, verbatim" becomes "`data-toast-
   container` is v4's testability handle (`58120a7fe`), the container is
   v4's polite live region"; `ui/toast.service.ts:30` and `:37` reworded the
   same way (three source comments). NEW `e2e/support/toasts.ts`:
   `export const toastStack = (page: Page) => page.locator('[data-toast-
   container] > div')`, `export const toastByClass = (page: Page, klass:
   'qt-toast-success' | 'qt-toast-error' | 'qt-toast-warning' | 'qt-toast-
   info') => page.locator(`[data-toast-container] .${klass}`)`, `export const
   toastContainer = (page: Page) => page.locator('[data-toast-container]')`
   (C9's helper, named as the contract says). The 37 lines in 19 files
   (the list above): a MECHANICAL rewrite `[role="toast-container"]` →
   `[data-toast-container]` on every line; where a file already has a local
   helper (`toast-flow.spec.ts:38-40`, `toast-open-rows-flow.spec.ts:97`,
   `wardrobe-copy-to-folder-flow.spec.ts:193`, `wardrobe-folders-flow.spec.
   ts:201`, `salon-send-failure-restore-flow.spec.ts:220`) the helper body
   becomes the import from `./support/toasts` — nothing else in those files
   moves (the cherry-pick must be a no-op merge for P4.D344's `salon-chain-
   pause-toast-flow` / `salon-paused-hold-flow` lines, its H11). `ui/toast.
   spec.ts:105, :107, :120, :139, :143` move to `[data-toast-container]` and
   the spec GAINS three cases: the container carries `role="status"` +
   `aria-live="polite"`; an error toast carries `role="alert"`; a success /
   warning / info toast carries NO `role` attribute (`getAttribute('role')
   === null`). Measured red first: the three new cases fail on main; after
   the rewrite `grep -rn 'role="toast-container"' apps/web` → 0. Run
   `e2e/toast-flow.spec.ts` and `e2e/toast-open-rows-flow.spec.ts` BY FILE
   against the shared server (they are the two that assert the container
   itself — `toast-flow.spec.ts:55` `toHaveCount(1)`). Commit, then record
   the sha in the lane record and the status header ("the toast commit:
   `<sha>`") so siblings can cherry-pick it by sha.

2. **NEW `apps/web/src/app/ui/dialog-focus.ts` — the `qtDialogFocus`
   directive (v4 `hooks/useDialogFocus.ts` whole).** `@Directive({ selector:
   '[qtDialogFocus]', host: { '(keydown)': 'onKeydown($event)' } })` with
   `qtDialogFocus = input<boolean>(true)` (v4's `isOpen`; a host that is
   only rendered while open leaves it at the default; the slide-over binds
   `[qtDialogFocus]="isOpen()"`) and `qtDialogTrap = input<boolean>(true)`
   (R2's "handler NOT attached" for P4.D359's floating primitive — the trap
   is a no-op when `false`; focus-in / restore still run). Export the
   selector constant `DIALOG_FOCUSABLE` = v4's `FOCUSABLE` byte for byte
   (the terminal's `moveFocusOutOf` reuses it, item 15). Semantics, in v4's
   order: the opener is captured SYNCHRONOUSLY when the open edge is seen —
   in the constructor for a default-`true` host (that runs before any child
   `viewChild` / `afterNextRender` focus call, v4's "during render"), and in
   an `effect` on the input's `false → true` edge otherwise — as `document.
   activeElement`; on open, ONE `requestAnimationFrame` focuses the host
   element unless `host.contains(document.activeElement)`; on close /
   destroy, `cancelAnimationFrame` then `opener.focus()` iff `opener.
   isConnected`; the keydown handler: Tab only; `querySelectorAll(DIALOG_
   FOCUSABLE)` empty → `preventDefault()` + return; `Shift+Tab` with
   `activeElement === first || activeElement === host` → `last.focus()`;
   `Tab` with `activeElement === last` → `first.focus()` (both
   `preventDefault`). The host element must carry `tabindex="-1"` (the
   directive asserts it in dev mode with a `console.warn` naming the
   element — a v5 guard, recorded). NEW `ui/dialog-focus.spec.ts` with the
   eight cases of survey §6.2 transcribed from the hook: focus moves to the
   host after a frame (`vi.useFakeTimers` + `requestAnimationFrame` stub, or
   `await new Promise(requestAnimationFrame)`); NOT when a descendant already
   holds focus; Tab from last → first; Shift+Tab from first → last;
   Shift+Tab from the HOST → last; a `disabled` button is excluded; zero
   focusables → `defaultPrevented`; destroy restores the opener when
   connected and does NOT throw when it was removed; `qtDialogTrap=false`
   leaves Tab alone. Red first: the spec file does not exist.

3. **NEW `apps/web/src/app/ui/activate-on-key.ts`** — `export function
   activateOnKey(e: KeyboardEvent): void` with v4's four lines (`if (e.key
   !== 'Enter' && e.key !== ' ') return; if (e.target !== e.currentTarget)
   return; e.preventDefault(); (e.currentTarget as HTMLElement).click()`)
   and v4's docblock (`:3-14`, incl. the two-themes sentence), plus a
   `[qtActivateOnKey]` directive wrapper (`host: { '(keydown)':
   'activateOnKey($event)' }`) for templates that prefer an attribute to a
   `(keydown)` binding — both exported; NEW `ui/activate-on-key.spec.ts`:
   Enter clicks, Space clicks, `a` does not, a bubbled key from a child
   (`target !== currentTarget`) does not, `defaultPrevented` on the handled
   keys.

    lane's primitives named).** Every sibling SPA lane applies these to the

      overlay) carries `role="dialog"` — `role="alertdialog"` where `ui/
      dialog-roles.table.ts` says so — `aria-modal="true"`, `aria-labelledby=
      "<titleId>"` with `id="<titleId>"` on the visible title element (a
      per-instance id: a module counter `qt-dialog-<n>`; there is no
      `UniqueId` injectable), `tabindex="-1"`, the shared directive
      `qtDialogFocus` (`ui/dialog-focus.ts`) for focus-in / Tab-trap /
      restore; Escape closes unless the host's busy gate says otherwise
      (keep v4's gate expression). For a `qt-modal` host: nothing — the
      primitive does it (bind `[closeOnEscape]` only where v4 gates it).
    - **R2 Non-modal floating surface** (`FloatingDialog`, P4.D359's
      `ui/floating-dialog.ts`): `role="dialog" aria-modal="false" aria-
      labelledby`, `tabindex="-1"`, `qtDialogFocus [qtDialogTrap]="false"`
      (focus in on open and back to the opener on close, NO Tab trap), close
      button `aria-label="Close"`, Escape inside closes it (topmost:
      `stopPropagation`), Escape elsewhere closes it only while `!document.
      querySelector('[aria-modal="true"]')`, both skipping `defaultPrevented`.
    - **R3 Icon-only buttons** carry v4's `aria-label` byte: `Close`,
      `Cancel`, `Dismiss message`, `Close gallery`, `` Remove alias ${alias}
      ``, `Up one folder`, `Toggle model list` (+ `aria-expanded`).
    - **R4 Visible label ↔ control:** `<label for="<id>">` + `id="<id>"` on
      the control (v4's slug after a per-instance prefix), or a `<label>`
      that wraps the control; never a bare `<span>` caption beside a
      control.

      ModelBehaviorCard / FieldSelectionStep / SuggestionCard /
      ContextCompression switch).

      has none, apply R4 and record `V5_ONLY_CONTROL`).
    - **R7 Switch:** `role="switch"` + `aria-checked` + a name (`aria-label`
      or `aria-labelledby`); the Prospero one is `type="button"` too.
    - **R8 Clickable non-button:** `role="button" tabindex="0" (keydown)=
      "activateOnKey($event)"` (`ui/activate-on-key.ts`) with `aria-
      expanded` (FileTable) / `aria-pressed` (gallery tile, quick-hide tags)

## The differential requirement, restated

This lane ships NO Rust and owns NO harness family; v4 shipped NO tests for
`eed72d60c` / `dff76ebef` / `10e09b7c3` (survey §0 #8), so there is no v4
code to drive and NO sweep-driver invocation is owed (`recipe_sweep.py` is
not run; the IMPORT-DEAD census of §R.4 (a) names nothing in this area —
`grep -rln 'lib/toast\|useDialogFocus\|ReplyAnnouncer\|activate-on-key'
harness/oracle` → nothing). The "oracle" is the ATTRIBUTE SET in the hunks,
and the equivalence evidence is the SPA tier's, three ways: (1) **the
transcribed semantics** — `ui/dialog-focus.spec.ts` / `ui/activate-on-key.
spec.ts` / `chat/reply-announcer.spec.ts` / the modal + slide-over cases
transcribe v4's hook / helper / component BEHAVIOUR case for case from the
hunk (the opener captured before a child focuses; `Shift+Tab` from the host;
the 300-char cut; the seed-once rule), each case red on main first; (2)
**the static tripwires** — `scripts/check-a11y-names.mjs` (every control
named, by v4's rule set) and `ui/dialog-roles.spec.ts` (every overlay's role
and name binding, from the committed table that mirrors `dff76ebef`'s
per-file list) — both read SOURCE, so a regression in any lane's later
edit fails `npm run lint` / `npm test`; (3) **the live judge** — the axe
sweep (Tier 2, ⚖) against the real server, pack × mode, diffed against a
committed known-violations file. The byte discipline is the same as a tier-1
string family: every `aria-label`, every `sr-only` sentence, every live-
region attribute is v4's byte from `git show <sha> -- <file>`, and the spec
asserts the byte. The silent-stale-pass discipline (ledger §5.2) maps to:
never add a spec case that passes on main before the edit (record the red),
never let the census allowlist carry a file that no sibling owns, never
`it.skip` a dialog-roles row without an `owner`. P4.D344's H2 line (H-in-1)
is proven by P4.D344's spec on the union, not here.

## Fixtures

- **Consumed:** the committed e2e chat fixture pair (`crates/quilltap-web/
  tests/fixtures/salon-{main,mount}.db`) through the shared global-setup
  server (`e2e/support/env.ts` `PORT = 4319`); the `characters-*` pair only
  if the a11y sweep needs its own instance (4357 / mock 45355). Nothing is
  rebuilt or widened; the KEYSTONE widens the `salon-*` pair (its `chats`
  columns) — your e2e runs on main's pair are unaffected (the SPA reads no
  new column here).
- **Delivered:** `e2e/support/toasts.ts` (the helper every later spec
  imports); `scripts/a11y-names-allowlist.json` (the sibling-owned list —
  the unifier empties it); `ui/dialog-roles.table.ts` (the committed table —
  the unifier drops the `owner` fields); `e2e/a11y/known-violations.json`
  (if Tier 2.1 lands — the unifier re-baselines it on the union, since
  siblings' new UI adds nodes; every row carries an `owner` or a rule
  reference so none is anonymous).
- **Regenerated by others:** none of yours. The help-ensure fixture is
  P4.D360's; `fresh_schema.json` the KEYSTONE's.
- **The committed-fixture rule** (widen through v4's REAL migrations, never
  rebuild a pair you do not own) is not exercised by this lane.

## Rulings made at planning (the planner's calls; the human may overrule before lane start — a STOP item if they do)

- ⚖ **R-1 — `@axe-core/playwright` as a NEW SPA dev dependency** (Deque,
  MPL-2.0; it pins its own `axe-core`; the SPA has no eslint / angular-eslint
  — adding those would be a larger decision). The planner's call (round R-N's
  last clause): **YES**, behind an optional `a11y` Playwright project that
  the default `npm run e2e` does not run (Tier 2.1). Alternative: refuse —
  then the static census + the dialog-roles vitest + the per-overlay specs
  are the whole gate (Tier 1 already carries them), and the item is
  recorded `NOT_LANDED(R-1 refused)`. The human rules before the lane
  reaches Tier 2; Tier 1 does not wait.
- **R-2 — `qt-modal` Escape CONVERGES on v4 (round R-N / lane-table R-K;
  the human may overrule).** `closeOnEscape = input(true)`; the `modal.ts:
  22-24` convention retired; every host that hand-rolls Escape converted so
  ONE owner of Escape remains per host; every v4-gated host bound. The
  alternative (keep the convention, record the divergence a third time) is
  rejected: v4's help text now documents Escape as user-facing behaviour
  (`help/appearance-settings.md:282-285`, P4.D360's), and the convention was
  already leaking (`save-image-dialog.ts:99`, `status-log.md:177492`,
  `:178642`).
- **R-3 — the Escape listener is DOCUMENT-level, bubble phase, no
  `stopPropagation`, skips `defaultPrevented`** — v4 `useClickOutside.ts:
  98-110`'s shape. Consequence (recorded as `MODAL_ESCAPE_NO_STOP_PROPAGATION`):
  stacked `qt-modal`s both close on one Escape, exactly as stacked v4
  `BaseModal`s do; a host that needs the inner-popover behaviour keeps its
  own capture-phase handler and binds `[closeOnEscape]="false"` (v4's
  `escapeCapture` opt-in). The `isInFloatingLayer` early return (`:101-103`)
  is P4.D359's — the modal's listener gains `if (isInFloatingLayer(event.
  target)) return;` at the union (H-out-6's companion; a one-line unifier
  wire — the unifier adds it when `ui/floating-layer.ts` exists).
- **R-4 — ONE focus directive for modal, slide-over, hand-rolled overlays
  AND P4.D359's floating primitive** (`qtDialogFocus`, with `qtDialogTrap`
  for R2's "handler not attached"). v4 has one hook; v5 has one directive.
  The alternative — a per-component copy, as `slide-over-panel.ts:131-199`
  is today — is the duplication P4.D300 / P4.D276 recorded and v4 itself
  removed in `58120a7fe`.
- **R-5 — the per-instance id is a module counter** (`qt-dialog-<n>`, the
  `subprompt-picker.ts:17` idiom); Angular has no `useId()`. A static id is
  acceptable ONLY for a provable singleton (the alert host, the shell) and
  is recorded per file.
- **R-6 — the static census accepts `title` as an icon-only button's name**
  (recorded `CENSUS_TITLE_COUNTS_AS_NAME`): the SPA names dozens of icon
  buttons by `title` alone; the WCAG survey's rule set is about CONTROLS
  (`input` / `select` / `textarea` / the four roles), and the axe sweep (if
  landed) is the stricter judge on buttons. Alternative: require `aria-
  label` on every icon-only button this round — rejected as a sweep no
  hunk asks for.
- **R-7 — a v5-only control with no v4 string takes R4, never an invented
  `aria-label`** (`V5_ONLY_CONTROL` rows). The byte rule is C9 R6's.
- **R-8 — where a v5 `aria-label` diverges from v4's named shape, v4's
  wins** (`document-pane.ts:251` → `Document source`; `participant-card.ts:
  273` → the `<label for>`; `visibility-section.ts`'s four → Tier 2). Already-
  converged items are NOT reds (Tier 3.4).
- **R-9 — Help stays a modal this round** (R1), because R-N scopes the
  floating primitive to the Brahma console; if the human widens the scope,
  Tier 2.6 moves the file to P4.D359.
- **R-10 — the toast commit is FIRST and self-contained** (the role, the
  helper, the 37 + 5 + 3 lines, nothing else), so any sibling can cherry-
  pick it by sha without pulling the directive or the modal change.
- **R-11 — the a11y sweep rides the shared server** (read-only pages); the
  reserved 4357 / 45355 pair is spent only on a measured need (recorded).
- **R-12 — `participant-card.ts` is wholly this lane's** (P4.D353's
  correction (b)); `details-tab.ts` likewise (H-out-9 keeps it so);
  `search/search-dialog.ts` is shared BY REGION with P4.D359 (this lane
  `:58-60` / `:69`; P4.D359 the document-listener guard) — the unifier
  unions two disjoint hunks.

run (§R.9's two-gate cap is left to the lanes that need it). Run `cargo
build --workspace` ONCE, after `npm run build` has produced `apps/web/dist`,
as a sanity check that the SPA build still embeds (record the exit code; a
failure is a finding, not yours to fix in a crate). SPA (`apps/web`, after

names.mjs` (exit 0; zero offenders outside the allowlist; every allowlist

    exist at the pin (`[Orchestrator] Storyteller chat — recording the
    operator post; a round will open`, `Tools above the turn's effect
    ceiling filtered from per-turn list`, `[Storyteller] Round housekeeping
    failed`) — do not port them. **The ceiling site:** v4 `resolveTurnEffect
    Ceiling(chat, chatSettings, storytellerCeiling)` (`turn-tools.service.ts:
    221-238` — non-autonomous → the storyteller ceiling; autonomous with
    policy ≠ `always_refuse` AND `runDestructiveToolsAllowed === 1` → the

    ceiling {chatId, policy, allowedAtRoom}` and `lowerToolEffect(…, 'write')`)
    passed INTO `buildTools` where `applyEffectCeilingToTools` (a PRIVATE fn
    in `streaming.service.ts:152-185`, NOT in `effects.ts` as the ledger
    says) is applied LAST, dropping every tool whose `maxEffectOfTool` is
    `destroy` — `delete_annotation` and `doc_delete_blob` join the three
    (**the withheld set widens 3 → 5**) — and the executor REFUSES a destroy
    call regardless (`tool-executor.ts:343-371`, after the mountPool /
    operatorSurface refusal, before any dispatch; INFO `Tool call refused:
    above the turn's effect ceiling`). **`DESTRUCTIVE_TOOL_NAMES`'s v5
    consumers** (all RETIRE under P4.D343): `services/tool_build.rs:757`
    (the three names), `:768 destructive_allowed`, `:774 filter_destructive_
    tools`, `:788 apply_autonomous_destructive_filter`, the P4.D234 INFO
    `Autonomous room: destructive tools filtered from per-turn list` at
    `:800`; the caller POST-step at `orchestrator.rs:2348-2370` (REPLACED by
    `resolve_turn_effect_ceiling` feeding `BuildToolsInput.effect_ceiling`);
    the harness grep `tool_build_equivalence.rs:312` and the row
    `p4d234_destructive_autonomous_mail_only` (`:317` — its premise
    "discard_mail is the ONE destructive tool" no longer holds). The
    `BUILT_IN_TOOL_EFFECTS` table has 71 entries (3 `none` / 29 `read` / 30
    `write` / 5 `destroy` / 4 computed with per-ceiling description notes);
    the Scriptorium search key is `search`, web search `search_web`.
    `toolChoice: 'none'` reaches the wire on FOUR providers only (nanogpt,
    deepseek, z-ai, openai-compatible; OpenRouter hard-codes `'auto'`;
    Anthropic / Google / OpenAI / Ollama / Grok never read it); `turnMode`
    is PATCH-ONLY (`setTurnMode`, `'rotation'` stored as NULL) and rides
    BESIDE `chat` on the PUT; `storyteller_rounds` is the first server-held
    transient table v5 creates at boot and truncates on `replace` restore
    (`delete_all.rs` `FORMAT3_MAIN_TABLES`) but never carries; a bid's
    `llm_logs` row has `messageId NULL` and never reaches the finalizer.
  - (g) **The intervention hold's position in a tool run** (pascal survey):
    awaited INSIDE the run service, BETWEEN the deal and the effects — v4
    `lib/pascal/run-service.ts:200-220` (after execute, before effects) → v5
    `pascal/run_service.rs` between `execute_custom_tool` (`:258-291`) and
    `apply_effects_for_run` (`:295-310`); downstream `toolTitle =
    presentation.title`, `announcerCharacterId = presentation.announcer ===

    LAST (after `callerParticipantId`); a `deal` whose `changedFields` is
    EMPTY is downgraded to `as-rolled` and records nothing. `mayHoldRun`:
    `!isIntervenable` → false SILENT; else each skip logs DEBUG `Custom tool
    run not held` with one of five reasons; an unreadable settings read →
    HOLD. The hold has NO timeout; first resolve wins; v4's `keepAlive` ping
    has NO v5 counterpart (ruling `HOLD_KEEP_ALIVE_NO_STREAM`); Stop vetoes
    through P4.D348's `TurnStopReach` (`ToolExecutionContext.turn_control`);
    `b02d902f3` DELETED the Re-ask refusal `This run's consult is not open to
    intervention` — at the pin Re-ask refuses ONLY a definition without `llm`
    (`This tool consults no oracle`, 400); `PascalMetaIn` (`db/chats_messages.
    rs:283-345`) silently DROPS unknown keys — P4.D345 adds ONE field

  - (h) **The keystone-memory facts the row overstated:** `keystone` is a
    FLAG, not a tier (R-E) — `bucketCandidates` skips `memory.keystone`
    rows AFTER `rowsLoaded++`, BEFORE the `CONSOLIDATED` branch; `isDemotable
    = hot && AUTO && !keystone`; `policyProtectionOf` answers `'keystone'`
    FIRST and `sparedBy`'s key order becomes `{keystone, manual, digest,
    cold}`; a keystone fold survivor takes NO footnotes and an EMPTY patch
    but keeps links + the reinforcement delta; `cascadeEmptiedDigests` keeps
    a keystone digest whose last member retires (DEBUG `Keystone digest kept
    though its last member retires`) and its `added` now counts only rows
    actually doomed (`if (!grew) break`) — port as shipped; `protection()`
    answers `{score: 1, protected: true, protectedBySource: true}`. In
    recall, "every digest is cut before any keystone" holds only INSIDE the
    inner circle (`innerCircleTrimOrder` sorts `Number(b.keystone) −
    Number(a.keystone)` first): present-character keystones are LIFTED into
    the circle at `tier −1` ahead of that character's digests, self
    keystones join the anchors, and `renderArchive` / `renderHead` render a
    keystone's `content` first (`summary` first otherwise). `KEYSTONES_PER_
    SUBJECT_MAX = 5`; the v4 quirk — `POST /memories` with `keystone: true`
    answers 400 `The memory was saved, but not as a keystone: <msg>` AFTER
    writing the row — is ported as the bytes (Q3). `keystone` appears in
    EVERY parsed Memory (between `consolidatedAt` and `createdAt`), so every
    byte pin of a memory row moves; `hydrateRawRows` coerces number / bigint
    / null to a boolean and DROPS a string cell with the WARN.
  - (i) **The WCAG ownership rule** (wcag survey §7.3): "the lane with the
    larger or structural hunk owns the file; the a11y lane records a HANDOFF
    naming the rule(s) R1–R12 and v4's bytes; the owner applies them in its
    own commit; the unifier checks the handoff list. Ties go to the feature
    lane (the a11y edits are mechanical)." Every sibling SPA lane applies
    rules **R1–R12** (the binding text is Shared contract C9) to the files it
    owns and records each as a `HANDOFF:` for P4.D358's census; P4.D358's
    static census script takes an allowlist for sibling-owned files, which
    the unifier empties. Already CONVERGED (not reds): the sidebar switches,
    `Allow Any Character`, the edit box, the composer `Message`, the
    slide-over's focus. The real gap: of 34 overlay components, 13 have no
    `role="dialog"`; `qt-modal` (77 hosts) carries `role` + `aria-modal` +
    the close label but NO `aria-labelledby` / `tabindex` / focus / Escape.

    135 → **137** at the pin (+`diagrams.md` 132 lines, +`storyteller-mode.md`

- **§R.5 — Log lines.** Every line this round ports or moves lands with a
  capture pin (the thread-scoped rig) AND its silence leg.
  - A line emitted INSIDE a `Db::write` closure runs on the writer thread and
    is INVISIBLE to the rig: run the home on the caller thread (memory note
    `capture-rig-writer-thread`) or use `global_capture`.
  - A `db::fallback` line renders the BARE SQLite / Zod message
    (`error_text`), never `sqlite error: …`.
  - A structured v4 field that is an array or object renders through the
    `…Json` file-layer convention; v4's camelCase keys are the keys; an
    `undefined` v4 field is OMITTED; child-logger bindings are fields on the
    line; `durationMs` and other wall-clock fields are MASKED.
  - Open before `captured_with`; a v4 catch UNREACHABLE through v4's real
    code is pinned NEGATIVE with a v5 fallback home.
  - **Migration lines follow the standing precedent** (P4.D249 / P4.D304 /
    P4.D315 / P4.D324, `host.rs`): v4's migration INFO / ERROR texts are
    NO-PORT and each boot ensure logs ONE v5-worded INFO per added column /
    table / index.

- **§R.7 — Red-first.**
  - **Measure red before touching source** — on unported `main` for the
    wave-0 lanes, on the `KEYSTONE` tip for its dependents (a contract
    refusal arm answering `not_available` IS the red). The lane record
    carries the red count per family. A test that passes before the fix is
    a test of nothing.
  - **Ruled divergences** are NAMED both-ways tables in the idiom of
    `FRESH_STORE_RESIDUAL` / `BOOT_CREATED_HISTORY_TABLE`, so a v4
    convergence trips them. This round's planned names: **P4.D345**
    `HOLD_KEEP_ALIVE_NO_STREAM` (v4's 15 s keep-alive ping — no v5
    counterpart by architecture), `ROUND_DROPS_MIN_EXCLUSIVE` (only if the
    corpus shows it); **P4.D354** `REFUSING_SEAM_LEAVES_PDF_UNEXTRACTED`
    (the backfill and the `_indexed` bridges leave a PDF / DOCX row at
    `'none'` while `DocumentTextExtractor::is_available()` is `false`; the
    REST path keeps v4's `failed` bytes); **P4.D347** `ECHO_SLICE_SURROGATE`
    + `SENTENCE_SLICE_SURROGATE` (a JS `slice` that cuts a surrogate pair —
    v5 rounds to the code-point boundary); **P4.D343** the dry scope's
    table-delta proof (whatever the `DryBuildContextSeams` wrapper does not
    cover is a recorded divergence, never a silent write) and `plugin_tool_
    effects` as NO-COUNTERPART; **P4.D350** `PUBLISH_AFTER_COMMIT` only if
    the human overrules its R-1 (v4 publishes at the write; v5 after the
    commit); **P4.D355** the explicit `terminal_sessions` DELETE on chat
    delete (v5's cascade stand-in on a generateDDL table with no FK — named
    in its record; R-R); **P4.D341** none owed (Q3's post-write 400 is
    ported as bytes); **P4.D352** an ADAPTER divergence recorded in the SPA
    (ProseMirror `Decoration.inline` for v4's CSS Custom Highlight API; the
    four `::highlight(name)` rules become class rules with the same tokens);
    **P4.D353** a Tier-3 code-block note only if R-B is overruled to (C).
  - **BY-DESIGN flips (ledger §5.4 — measure, then retire / re-aim, never
    delete unread):** the #120 convergence pins (**P4.D356** — the three
    `s_get_cheap_llm_bad_strategy` / `_bad_uuid` / `_non_object` rows
    compared whole at `settings_routes_equivalence.rs:591-599`, the unit test

    (**P4.D360**). ONE convergence row in the span: `7789ebed6` (#120 — this
    port's F-1 filing); bugs 258–269 are v4-originated.
- **§R.8 — The unifier's accumulation.** Each lane's record states its
  `Cargo.toml` versions at close; the unifier takes the HIGHEST and bumps +1
  for the union commit; `Cargo.lock` follows. The SPA: the highest of the
  SPA lanes, +1. CHANGELOG entries and `status-log.md` lane records
  union-merge (append-only).
- **§R.9 — One machine.**
  - **Workspace gates:** at most TWO Rust workspace gates at once. Count them
    with ONE `ps -ax -o pid,etime,command | grep "cargo test --workspace" |
    grep -v grep`; if two run, queue yours in ONE background command waiting
    on the siblings' PIDs (`while kill -0 <pidA> && kill -0 <pidB>; do sleep
    20; done; sleep $((RANDOM % 20)); <re-count once; if still two, wait on
    those PIDs>; cargo test …`), never on a pattern (memory notes
    `no-pattern-kill-on-shared-machine`, `parallel-lane-shared-resources`
    §2 — co-waiters all fire on one exit; re-count after the wait).
  - **Sweeps and Playwright:** one sweep at a time (announced); one
    Playwright run at a time (lanes by file only; the full suite is the
    unifier's). New own-instance e2e files' server ports are unique per
    lane (4319–4340, 4343–4350, 4352 and mocks 45301–45304, 45340–45348 are
    taken): **P4.D344 4353 / mock 45351**, **P4.D346 4354 / mock 45352**,
    **P4.D352 4355 / mock 45353**, **P4.D353 4356 / mock 45354**, **P4.D358
    4357 / mock 45355**, **P4.D359 4358 / mock 45356**, **P4.D355 4359 /
    mock 45357** (only if a terminal e2e is added). A lane that only edits
    an EXISTING spec takes no port.
  - **Long jobs:** `run_in_background: true`, full log in a lane-private
    file, awaited by the completion notification — NEVER a `pgrep`/`grep`
    poll loop (CLAUDE.md), never `tail -N` on a gate log. A gate reads its
    oracles and source at RUN time: no regen and no source edit while one
    runs (memory note `lane-gate-hygiene`).
  - **Targets:** clone main's `target/` before the first build (`cp -cR`;
    `/carryout` rule 8 — same volume only, memory note
    `apfs-clone-same-volume-only`); main's `target/` was warmed by the
    `/setupphase` chain on 2026-10-29 at the orders' commit (the test /
    clippy set the lanes clone; the release bins were swept — an
    own-instance e2e that prefers `target/release/*` must delete the stale
    release bins from its clone or build release, memory note
    `lane-spa-env-node-modules-and-release-bins`); a stacked lane may clone
    its parent lane's worktree target instead, when that lane is not
    mid-build.
  - **`apps/web/dist/`:** a fresh worktree has none — build it before any
    workspace clippy / test. **`apps/web/node_modules`:** `npm ci` in the
    lane (a cloned copy fails every vitest spec with NG0203).
  - **Concurrency:** the cap is the two-gate rule, not the lane count;
    twenty lanes do NOT all run at once (§S.0 waves). Disk at planning:
    144 GiB free on the repo volume with `target/` at 347 GB BEFORE the
    warm sweep — the human sizes the waves from the post-sweep numbers in
    the hand-off report.

- **§R.11 — Rulings for the human.** Each order's "Rulings made at
  planning" are the planner's calls; the human may overrule any before lane
  start (an overruled ruling is a STOP item for that lane). The round-level
  ones to read FIRST (none is yet approved — the human approves or
  overrules at the orders' hand-off):
  - ⚖ **THE NPM CACHE GAP (vendor survey §F.4 — a PRE-ROUND BLOCKER):** the
    human's live checkout is at `4.12.0-dev.5` (`openai` 7.45.1, the v13
    driver), so the npm cache never saw the pin's versions — 44 of the 188
    tarballs the pin's lock adds / moves are `ENOTCACHED`; `npm ci --offline`
    at the pin FAILS as written. **The planner's call: ONE online `npm ci
    --no-audit --no-fund` in a pin worktree before any lane starts** (the
    human, or the first lane with the human's go-ahead), which populates the
    cache for every lane's `--offline` rebuild; record the resulting
    `node_modules/.package-lock.json` stamp. The alternative (the live
    `node_modules` symlink) runs every oracle on 7.45.1 / 1.5.21 / the v13
    driver and is REFUSED by this round. Every order's pin recipe says:
    verify `npm ci --offline` succeeds; if `ENOTCACHED`, STOP and report.
  - **THE PIN (human, 2026-10-09, standing)** — §R.1 / §R.2: `236c04e35`,
    v4's 4.11.0 code, NOT v4 HEAD at planning (the planner's call: the two
    newer commits carry an unruled driver / SQLite move; a released
    baseline is nameable; both go to the next round with the ruling).
  - **R-A — ONE `fresh_schema.json` re-dump at the pin, owned by the
    keystone (P4.D341)**, carrying the SEVENTEENTH to TWENTY-FIRST moves;
    no other lane re-dumps; `migration_indexes.json` moves ONCE in the same

    instance with the MIGRATION's DDL verbatim + both indexes (ruling Q2 —

    `@hpcc-js/wasm-graphviz` 1.30.0, `svg2roughjs` 3.2.3, `dompurify`
    3.4.17 — the first client runtime deps since ProseMirror / KaTeX):
    measured — `mermaid@12.2.0` (122 MB unpacked, 23 deps incl. `d3`,
    `cytoscape`, `elkjs`, `katex ^0.18.0` — compatible with v5's pinned
    0.18.4; lazy-loaded, so the initial bundle is unaffected, but `npm ci`
    and the lockfile grow by hundreds of packages), `@hpcc-js/wasm-graphviz@
    1.30.0` (2.0 MB, zero deps, wasm inlined), `svg2roughjs@3.2.3` (1.9 MB),
    `dompurify@3.4.17` (1.6 MB; also mermaid's transitive); all four load by
    dynamic `import()` in v4 → Angular's esbuild builder emits lazy chunks
    OUTSIDE the `initial` budget (`angular.json:39-44`); wasm inlined — no
    `.wasm` fetch (`static_serve.rs:34` maps `application/wasm` anyway);
    Tauri's CSP is `null` (`tauri.conf.json:19`) and `quilltap-web` sets no
    page CSP, so no `'wasm-unsafe-eval'`. Options: **(A) port whole** with the
    four (v4 parity; the first `ng build` is the proof that esbuild splits
    `import('mermaid')`); **(B) mermaid + DOMPurify only** — saves 3.9 MB of
    lazy weight and two deps, loses `dot` / `graphviz` / `svg` fences (shown
    as code with a loud note) and the hand-drawn Graphviz; **(C) defer

- **§R.14 — Stacking rules.** Each named stacking commit — `KEYSTONE`
  (P4.D341), `CONTEXT` (P4.D347), `SPINE` (P4.D348), `CATALOG` (P4.D350's
  FIRST commit), `FILES` (P4.D354) — is ONE commit whose subject starts with
  its name; the owner records the sha in its status header ON ITS LANE
  BRANCH the moment it lands. A dependent lane — P4.D347 / P4.D342 / P4.D351
  / P4.D356 from `KEYSTONE`; P4.D348 from `CONTEXT`; P4.D343 / P4.D345 from
  `SPINE` (which is on `CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`); P4.D349 from `CATALOG`;
  P4.D355 from `FILES` — finds the sha per `/carryout` rule 2 (`git log --all
  --format='%h %s' | grep -E '^[0-9a-f]+ <NAME>:'`, confirmed against the
  parent's lane-branch copy of the order) and branches from it — never from
  `main`, never a guessed sha; if the parent has not recorded it, STOP and
  report. A stacked lane may PRE-START from `main` on work that calls none
  of its parent's contract names — P4.D342's prompt bytes (the four
  extraction ladders + the consolidation tail) + the inner-circle resolver
  (`services/inner_circle.rs`, the tuning knobs); P4.D343's `tools/effects.rs`
  + `pascal/tool_effect.rs` + `StreamParams.tool_choice` + the `tool-build`
  re-aim (its unit E) + the pure `storyteller/*` modules; P4.D345's hold
  registry + eligibility / overrides / recompute / spans + the engine's
  `ReplayInputs` + the ONE corpus regen + the schema re-vendor; P4.D351's
  `services/annotations.rs` + `template_prompt_hint.rs` ports; P4.D356's
  NanoGPT half + the Tier R verify; P4.D349's `pronoun-presets` /
  `pronoun-guidance` ports + the image-prompt `humanoid` threading; P4.D355's
  `db/log_entries.rs` + the terminal host; P4.D348's `operator_post.rs` /
  `whisper_send.rs` / `turn_control.rs` additions and the funnel recorder;
  P4.D347 has no pre-start (its first unit widens `RawMessage`, which rides
  the keystone's row shapes) — rebasing onto the parent's commit before the
  first commit that does call a contract name; that rebase is the one
  sanctioned exception to "no rebase mid-lane". Two sibling-stacked parts
  (not lane-named commits): P4.D346's grammar-twin part (`pascal/custom-tool-
  types.ts` + the corpus copy + the spec header) stacks on P4.D345's Tier-1
  commit, or authors blind and lets the unifier run `custom-tool-types.
  corpus.spec.ts` — the order states which; P4.D359's chips stack on
  P4.D353's Tier-1 commit (the `message-content.ts` hook). Every other lane
  cuts from `main` at the orders' commit.

## §S — Unifier wires (what no single lane can do)

- **§S.0 — Waves (the recommended execution; the two-gate cap rules the
  machine, not the lane count).**
  - **Wave 0, from the orders' commit, at once:** P4.D341 (→ `KEYSTONE`,
    the Opus / Fable tier), P4.D350 (→ `CATALOG` as its FIRST commit, then
    its own units), P4.D354 (→ `FILES`), and in parallel P4.D360, P4.D357,
    P4.D358. That is six worktrees.
  - **Wave 1, as slots free (still from the orders' commit):** the five SPA
    feature lanes P4.D344, P4.D346, P4.D352, P4.D353, P4.D359 (each authors
    against the contracts with gated beats; P4.D346's grammar-twin part and
    P4.D359's chips wait for their sibling's Tier-1 commit).
  - **Wave A, from `KEYSTONE`:** P4.D347 (→ `CONTEXT`) FIRST, then P4.D342,
    P4.D351, P4.D356 (its NanoGPT + Tier R half may have PRE-STARTED from
    `main`). **From `CATALOG`:** P4.D349 — FIRST among the independents
    (R-H). **From `FILES`:** P4.D355.

  new baseline — v4 HEAD is already past it); the schema state (moves
  17–21 LANDED in ONE re-dump; `migration_indexes.json` +3); the LIVE-DATA
  block (vendor survey §I, sharpened by the keystone survey — **a v5 at
  `236c04e35` reads and writes:** `memories.keystone` (the 17th move, the
  migration's `INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` + the partial index `idx_memories_
  keystone` as ONE boot ensure), `chats.turnMode` / `storytellerInviteFilter`,
  `chat_messages.storytellerBid`, the NEW table `storyteller_rounds` (with
  its FK + both indexes as ensures), `llm_logs.storytellerRoundId` (the
  FIRST llm-logs column ensure v5 has needed — before the structural
  verify), `chat_settings.customToolIntervention` (default ON), `roleplay_
  templates.unmarkedStyle` / `markdownAsterisks` — and runs `778b97b2b`'s
  every-boot seed that re-encodes the built-in templates' `narrationDelimiters`
  as JSON and inserts the Novel template with it `null`. **The first v5
  boot of a pre-`8cb584eff` Friday copy ADDS the eight columns + one table
  (column-gated, v4's shapes, every ensure UNSTAMPED) and REWRITES the
  built-in templates' delimiter cells `*` → `"*"`; a copy v4 migrated sees
  NO-OP ensures and a no-op seed (measure: the seed must be idempotent
  against v4's own output).** **The THREE vault-key ERASURE hazards RETIRE**
  once P4.D349 lands: `render_physical_prompts_json` carries `humanoid` (a
  v4 `"humanoid": false` survives a v5 physical write and the P4.82
  backfill respects it), `PropertiesJson` carries `canDrawDiagrams` /
  `diagramStyle`, `build_wardrobe_item_file` carries `pronounGuidance`;
  until that lane is on the union build, **no v5 write to a character v4
  edited past `6588972d5` / `b27e5089a` / `eb90d9ed8`** (reads stay safe).
  **The Log is carried** (`cc5f2347c`, P4.D355): a v5 backup writes
  `data/log-entries.json`, a `replace` restore / delete-all clears
  `log_entries`, a `new-account` restore remaps `id` / `chatId` /
  `characterId`; `.qtap` still omits it; a pre-round v5 backup has no Log
  file — restoring it on the union leaves the Log empty (expected).
  **Keystones protected:** maintenance, demotion, consolidation, fold and
  chat-wipe skip `keystone = 1` rows; recall lifts them into the circle; a
  v5 backup / `.qtap` carries the column; a pre-round v5 build must NOT
  run maintenance on a copy v4 keystoned. **Terminals:** kill sends SIGHUP
  then SIGKILL after 3 s; a deleted chat terminates its shells (and v5
  deletes its `terminal_sessions` rows explicitly); the daily sweep unlinks
  orphaned transcripts older than 1 h (`85c002822`, P4.D355). **Settings:**
  the chat-settings PUT refuses unknown keys with a 400 (`3b8b6f42b`) — a
  v5 SPA caller that echoed a GET row back now fails (P4.D356's census).
  **Files:** ingest fills `doc_mount_blobs.extractedText*` for native-text
  uploads and a boot backfill fills old rows (`40fab1c54`, P4.D354 — a DATA
  WRITE on first boot of any copy with uploads: measure the row count; PDF
  / DOCX rows stay `'none'` under the refusing seam). **The general rule
  BITES: a v5 build from BEFORE this round must not open an instance this
  round's ensures have altered — the owed dogfood pass runs with the UNION
  build.** **NOT this round (`8832cfd20`, `bf7a457f6`):** v4 HEAD links
  SQLite3MC 2.4.0 / SQLite 3.53.4; v5 stays 2.3.5 / 3.53.2 — the first v5
  open of a post-`8832cfd20` Friday copy is the measurement; the iCloud
  placeholder scan divergence stands as recorded. **Standing:** no v5 build
  against live Friday; dogfood a COPY; do not round-trip v4-written
  characters through v5 writes while comparing with v4); the dependency
  facts (`openai` 7.45.0, `@openrouter/sdk` 1.5.9, `pdfjs-dist` 6.4.299,
  `pdf-parse` removed, `@quilltap/plugin-types` 2.9.0; the four client deps
  `mermaid` 12.2.0 / `@hpcc-js/wasm-graphviz` 1.30.0 / `svg2roughjs` 3.2.3 /
  `dompurify` 3.4.17 if R-B lands them; `@axe-core/playwright` if R-N does);
  `help/` 137; `docs/v4` 518.
- **§S.7 — The docs.** CLAUDE.md's Status bullet and "Oracle baseline"

- **§S.9 — The gated beats flip LIVE.** P4.D344 / P4.D346 / P4.D352 /
  P4.D353 / P4.D359 author their Playwright beats behind gate constants
  (`const P4D343_SERVER_LANDED = false` etc.), with throwaway stand-ins
  deleted at the flip. On the union the unifier flips each to `true`,
  deletes the stand-ins, runs each touched spec by file (alone; 4353–4359
  free), then the full suite. A red is root-caused against the server logs
  before it is accepted as the recorded timing class.

- **C2 — the Storyteller wire W1 (P4.D343 builds, P4.D344 consumes;
  `crates/quilltap-core/src/api/types.rs` + `apps/web/src/app/core/core-
  contract.ts`):** `Request::ChatUpdate { …, turn_mode: Option<Option<Value>>
  }` (`double_option`, serde key `turnMode`, a sibling of `chat`; absent →
  untouched; `"rotation"` / `"storyteller"` → `set_turn_mode` (+ `abandon_
  storyteller_round(chat_id, "abandoned")` on leaving); `null` or anything
  else → `BadRequest "Validation error"`, nothing written) ↔ `ChatUpdate
  Request.turnMode?: 'rotation' | 'storyteller'`; the bag key `chat.
  storytellerInviteFilter: boolean` (non-boolean → the whole PUT refused).
  `Request::ChatStorytellerRound { chat_id }` → `Response::ChatStoryteller
  Round(Value)` = `{ "round": StorytellerRound | null }`. `Request::Chat
  StorytellerRun { chat_id, round_id: String, participant_ids: Vec<String>,
  order: Option<String>, keep_order: Option<bool> }` (keys `roundId`,
  `participantIds`, `order` default `"sequential"`, `keepOrder`; a non-enum
  `order` → `BadRequest "Invalid request"`) → an EMPTY reply (`Response::Ok`
  / `Null` — the web edge answers the SSE stream; `{ ended }` would be extra
  information v4 never answers); refusals `Conflict` 409 `That round is no
  longer open`, `NotFound` 404 `That seat is not on this round` / `Chat not
  found`, `BadRequest` 400 `Choose at least one bid, or let the round lie` /
  `Only a seat that bid to speak can be chosen` / `A Destroy bid must be
  chosen on its own`. `Request::ChatStorytellerAsk { chat_id, round_id,
  participant_id }` → `Response::ChatStorytellerAsk(Value)` = `{ "bid":
  StorytellerBid }` (the ANSWERED card). `Request::ChatStorytellerLie {
  chat_id, round_id }` → `{ "closed": true }`. Frames on `EventPayload::Chat
  (ChatEvent)`: `{"storytellerRound": StorytellerRound}`, `{"storytellerBid":
  {"roundId", "bid": StorytellerBid}}`, `{"roundOpen": true, "roundId"}`,
  `chainComplete` with `reason: "round_open"` (the literal; no enum change);
  a concurrent turn's frames carry `"participantId"` (when the frame lacked

- **C9 — the WCAG rules R1–R12 (P4.D358 writes; EVERY SPA lane applies to
  the files it owns and records a HANDOFF for the census):**
  - **R1 Modal dialog surface** (`dff76ebef`): the dialog CARD (never the
    overlay) carries `role="dialog"` — `role="alertdialog"` where the
    dialog-roles table says so — `aria-modal="true"`, `aria-labelledby=
    "<titleId>"` with `id="<titleId>"` on the visible title element (a
    per-instance id: `inject(UniqueId)` if the SPA has one, else a module
    counter `qt-dialog-<n>`); `tabindex="-1"`; the shared focus directive
    (`qtDialogFocus`, P4.D358's `ui/dialog-focus.ts`) for focus-in / Tab-trap
    / restore; Escape closes unless the host's busy gate says otherwise (keep
    v4's gate expression). For a `qt-modal` host: nothing — the primitive
    does it.
  - **R2 Non-modal floating surface** (`FloatingDialog`): `role="dialog"
    aria-modal="false" aria-labelledby`, `tabindex="-1"`, focus in on open
    and back to the opener on close, NO Tab trap, close button `aria-label=
    "Close"`, Escape inside closes it (topmost: `stopPropagation`), Escape
    elsewhere closes it only while `!document.querySelector('[aria-modal=
    "true"]')`, both skipping `defaultPrevented`.
  - **R3 Icon-only buttons** carry v4's `aria-label` byte: `Close`, `Cancel`,
    `Dismiss message`, `Close gallery`, `` Remove alias ${alias} ``, `Up one
    folder`, `Toggle model list` (+ `aria-expanded`).
  - **R4 Visible label ↔ control:** `<label for="<id>">` + `id="<id>"` on the
    control; never a bare `<span>` caption beside a control.
  - **R5 Heading-captioned control:** `aria-labelledby="<headingId>"` with
    `id` on the heading (ProfilesTab / ImageGenerationCard / ModelBehaviorCard
    / FieldSelectionStep / SuggestionCard / ContextCompression switch).
  - **R6 Placeholder-only / caption-less control:** `aria-label` with v4's
    string (never invent a string; where v4 has none, do not add one).
  - **R7 Switch:** `role="switch"` + `aria-checked` + a name (`aria-label` or
    `aria-labelledby`); the Prospero one is `type="button"` too.
  - **R8 Clickable non-button:** `role="button" tabindex="0" (keydown)=
    "activateOnKey($event)"` (P4.D358's `ui/activate-on-key.ts`) with
    `aria-expanded` (FileTable) / `aria-pressed` (gallery tile, quick-hide
    tags) where v4 sets them. Prefer a real `<button>` where the layout
    allows (v4 kept spans only inside `<tr>` / heading contexts and under
